---

[dict.2.ability]
Ability is being able to do a thing.

[dict.2.absolute]
Absolute means all of a thing and not only part of it.

[dict.2.absolutely]
Absolutely means with nothing left out.

[dict.2.absorb]
To absorb a thing is to take it in.

[dict.2.academy]
An academy is a school.

[dict.2.access]
Access is a way to get to a thing or into a place.

[dict.2.accident]
An accident is a bad thing that happens when nobody wanted it to.

[dict.2.according]
According means as was said, or in a way that matches.

[dict.2.account]
What someone says happened, told in order.

[dict.2.accuracy]
Accuracy is being right and not off by much.

[dict.2.accurate]
Accurate means exactly right, with no mistakes.

[dict.2.acid]
An acid is a substance that eats into some things it touches.

[dict.2.acidic]
Acidic means of an acid or like an acid.

[dict.2.act]
To act is to do something, and an act is one thing that someone does.

[dict.2.action]
An action is something that someone does.

[dict.2.active]
Active means doing something.

[dict.2.activity]
An activity is something that people do, often for fun or to learn.

[dict.2.actual]
Actual means real.

[dict.2.actually]
Actually means really, or in fact.

[dict.2.add]
To add is to put one amount with another to make a bigger amount.

[dict.2.adjacent]
Adjacent means next to something.

[dict.2.administrative]
Administrative means about the work of running a group.

[dict.2.adult]
An adult is a person who is not a child.

[dict.2.advantage]
An advantage is something that helps you do better.

[dict.2.advisory]
An advisory is a report that tells people about a danger, such as a coming storm, and what they should do.

[dict.2.aerosol]
An aerosol is a very small bit of a thing in the air.

[dict.2.affect]
To affect something is to change it or to act on it.

[dict.2.affection]
Affection is a warm feeling for a person.

[dict.2.afternoon]
Afternoon is the part of the day after the middle of the day.

[dict.2.afterward]
Afterward means at a later time.

[dict.2.age]
Age is how long a thing has been alive.

[dict.2.agency]
An agency is a group that does a kind of work for people.

[dict.2.ago]
Ago means before now, as in two days ago.

[dict.2.agricultural]
Agricultural means of farms and of growing food.

[dict.2.aircraft]
An aircraft is a thing that flies in the air.

[dict.2.airflow]
Airflow is air moving from one place to another.

[dict.2.alarm]
An alarm is a sound that says something bad is happening.

[dict.2.alcohol]
Alcohol is the name of a group of clear liquids that burn, and one kind of it is in some drinks.

[dict.2.alert]
Alert means ready and watching.

[dict.2.algae]
Algae are very small plants that live in water.

[dict.2.algal]
Algal means of algae.

[dict.2.algorithm]
An algorithm is a list of steps for doing a thing.

[dict.2.almost]
Almost means very close to all of something, but not all of it.

[dict.2.aloft]
Aloft means up in the air.

[dict.2.already]
Already means before now.

[dict.2.alternative]
An alternative is another thing that you can choose in place of the first one.

[dict.2.although]
Although means even though, as in we went out although it was cold.

[dict.2.altitude]
Altitude is how high something is above the ground.

[dict.2.altocumulus]
Altocumulus is cloud at middle height in small piles or rolls.

[dict.2.altogether]
Altogether means all of them counted as one.

[dict.2.altostratus]
Altostratus is a gray sheet of cloud at middle height.

[dict.2.amount]
An amount is how much there is of a thing.

[dict.2.analysis]
An analysis is a careful look at the parts of a thing.

[dict.2.angle]
An angle is the corner made where two straight lines meet.

[dict.2.annual]
Annual means once in a year.

[dict.2.annually]
Annually means once each year.

[dict.2.antenna]
An antenna is a long, thin piece of metal that sends or receives sound and pictures through the air.

[dict.2.anvil]
An anvil is a heavy metal block that a person hits hot metal on.

[dict.2.anywhere]
Anywhere means in any place.

[dict.2.apartment]
An apartment is a home that is part of a larger building.

[dict.2.apparent]
Apparent means looking real, whether it is or not.

[dict.2.appearance]
The appearance of something is the way it looks.

[dict.2.appliance]
An appliance is a machine that does a job in the home.

[dict.2.application]
An application is a way of using a thing to do a job.

[dict.2.apply]
To apply a thing is to put it on something.

[dict.2.approach]
To approach something is to move nearer to it.

[dict.2.appropriate]
Appropriate means right for the time and the place.

[dict.2.approximate]
Approximate means close but not exact.

[dict.2.approximately]
Approximately means close to, but not exactly.

[dict.2.aquatic]
Aquatic means living in water.

[dict.2.area]
An area is a part of a place or of a surface.

[dict.2.array]
An array is a set of things put together in lines.

[dict.2.arrive]
To arrive is to get to a place.

[dict.2.arrow]
An arrow is a thin stick with a sharp point at its front end, or a mark shaped like one that points the way.

[dict.2.art]
Art is a picture or other made thing that a person makes to be looked at.

[dict.2.artwork]
An artwork is one piece of art.

[dict.2.ash]
Ash is the gray dust left after a fire.

[dict.2.ashamed]
Ashamed means feeling bad about something you did.

[dict.2.assume]
To assume a thing is to take it as true without checking.

[dict.2.asteroid]
An asteroid is a rock in space that goes around the sun.

[dict.2.astronaut]
An astronaut is a person who travels in space.

[dict.2.astronomer]
An astronomer is a scientist who studies the stars, the planets and other things in space.

[dict.2.atmosphere]
The atmosphere is the air all around the earth.

[dict.2.atmospheric]
Atmospheric means of the air.

[dict.2.atom]
An atom is a very small part of a thing.

[dict.2.attack]
To attack is to try to hurt, and an attack is that try.

[dict.2.attention]
Attention is looking at one thing and not at the others.

[dict.2.attic]
An attic is the room at the top of a house.

[dict.2.attract]
To attract is to pull a thing toward you.

[dict.2.author]
An author is the person who wrote a thing.

[dict.2.automobile]
An automobile is a car.

[dict.2.autumn]
The time of year when leaves fall from trees and the air gets cool.

[dict.2.availability]
Availability is how easy a thing is to get or to use.

[dict.2.available]
Available means here and able to be used.

[dict.2.average]
The average of some numbers is the one number they would each be if the whole amount were shared out between them in equal parts.

[dict.2.aviation]
Aviation is the work of flying in the air.

[dict.2.awareness]
Awareness is knowing what is around you.

[dict.2.axis]
An axis is a straight line that something turns around, like the line from the top to the bottom of the earth.

[dict.2.background]
The background of a picture or a view is the part that is far behind the things at the front.

[dict.2.bacteria]
Bacteria are very small living things.

[dict.2.balance]
When two sides hold the same amount, so one is not more than the other.

[dict.2.bank]
A raised place of earth by a river or lake.

[dict.2.bar]
A bar is a long hard piece of metal or wood.

[dict.2.barb]
A barb is a sharp point that sticks back on a hook.

[dict.2.bare]
Bare means with nothing covering it.

[dict.2.bark]
Bark is the hard cover on the outside of a tree.

[dict.2.barn]
A barn is a large house for animals and for the food they eat.

[dict.2.barometer]
A barometer is a tool that measures how hard the air presses.

[dict.2.barrel]
A barrel is a big round container that holds a liquid such as oil.

[dict.2.barrier]
A barrier is something that stops you going through.

[dict.2.base]
A base is the bottom part that a thing stands on.

[dict.2.basic]
Basic means the other side from acidic for a liquid.

[dict.2.basin]
A basin is a low part of the land that water runs into.

[dict.2.basketball]
Basketball is a game in which you throw a ball through a high ring.

[dict.2.bat]
A bat is a small animal that flies at night.

[dict.2.bathtub]
A bathtub is a large thing you sit in when you wash.

[dict.2.bathymetry]
Bathymetry is the measuring of how deep water is.

[dict.2.battery]
A battery is a thing that keeps electricity in it to use later.

[dict.2.bay]
A bay is a part of the sea with land around much of it.

[dict.2.beach]
A beach is the flat land at the shore where you can walk.

[dict.2.beam]
A beam is a line of light.

[dict.2.beast]
A large animal that is not a person.

[dict.2.beat]
To beat a thing is to hit it again and again.

[dict.2.beautiful]
Beautiful is when a thing is very good to look at.

[dict.2.beauty]
Beauty is what makes something good to look at or to hear.

[dict.2.beaver]
A beaver is an animal that lives by water and cuts down trees.

[dict.2.bee]
A bee is a small flying insect that goes from flower to flower to get food.

[dict.2.beg]
To beg is to ask hard for something you need.

[dict.2.behave]
To behave is to act in some way.

[dict.2.behold]
To behold a thing is to look at it with care.

[dict.2.belt]
A belt is a long piece a person can wear around the middle of the body.

[dict.2.beneath]
Beneath means under or below something.

[dict.2.berry]
A berry is a small, soft, round fruit with many seeds inside.

[dict.2.beyond]
Beyond means on the far side of something, or past it.

[dict.2.bicycle]
A bicycle is a thing with two wheels that you use to move with your feet.

[dict.2.billion]
A billion is a thousand times a million.

[dict.2.biodiesel]
Biodiesel is diesel made from plant oil or animal fat.

[dict.2.biofuel]
A biofuel is a fuel made from plants or from animal waste.

[dict.2.biogas]
Biogas is a gas that bacteria make from waste, and it can be burned as a fuel.

[dict.2.biological]
Biological means of living things.

[dict.2.biomass]
Biomass is plant and animal material that people burn or use to make fuel.

[dict.2.birdie]
A birdie is a small bird.

[dict.2.bit]
A bit is a small piece or a small amount of something.

[dict.2.blade]
A blade is a flat or sharp part of a tool or a machine.

[dict.2.blank]
Blank means empty, with nothing on it.

[dict.2.blend]
To blend is to mix things well together.

[dict.2.bless]
To bless a person is to wish the person good.

[dict.2.blind]
Blind means not able to see.

[dict.2.blood]
Blood is the red liquid that moves inside the body.

[dict.2.bloom]
A bloom is a flower, and to bloom is to open into a flower.

To twit is to make fun of a person for a small thing.

[dict.2.type]
A type is a kind.

[dict.2.typical]
Typical means of the usual kind.

[dict.2.typically]
Typically means in the usual way.

[dict.2.ugly]
Ugly means not good to look at.

[dict.2.ultraviolet]
Ultraviolet light is a kind of light from the sun that people cannot see and that can burn the skin.

[dict.2.unchanged]
Unchanged means still the same as before.

[dict.2.underground]
Underground means under the ground.

[dict.2.unhappy]
Unhappy means not happy.

[dict.2.unhealthy]
Unhealthy means bad for the body.

[dict.2.uniform]
Uniform means the same all through, and a uniform is the same clothes worn by a group.

[dict.2.unique]
Unique means the only one of its kind.

[dict.2.universal]
Universal means true of all things.

[dict.2.universe]
The universe is all that there is.

[dict.2.unless]
Unless means if not, or except if.

[dict.2.unstable]
Unstable means likely to change, or likely to fall.

[dict.2.update]
To update something is to make it new by adding the latest facts.

[dict.2.updraft]
An updraft is air moving upward.

[dict.2.upgrade]
To upgrade something is to make it better or newer.

[dict.2.upon]
Upon means on, or on top of.

[dict.2.upper]
Upper means higher up, or nearer the top.

[dict.2.upstream]
Upstream means toward where a stream begins, against the flow.

[dict.2.upward]
Upward means toward a higher place.

[dict.2.upwelling]
Upwelling is deep water rising toward the top of the sea.

[dict.2.urban]
Urban means of a city.

[dict.2.urbanization]
Urbanization is the change of open land into city land.

[dict.2.useful]
Useful is when a thing helps you do what you want.

[dict.2.usual]
Usual means happening most of the time.

[dict.2.usually]
Usually means most of the time.

[dict.2.utility]
A utility is a company that sells power, water or gas to homes.

[dict.2.utter]
To utter is to speak, or to make a sound.

[dict.2.valley]
A valley is the low land between hills.

[dict.2.valuable]
Valuable means that people want it very much.

[dict.2.value]
The value of something is how much it is wanted, or how much it would cost.

[dict.2.vapor]
Vapor is a gas that came from a liquid, such as water in the air.

[dict.2.variation]
A variation is a change from what is usual.

[dict.2.variety]
A variety is a set of different things of one kind, or one such kind.

[dict.2.various]
Various means of many different kinds.

[dict.2.vary]
To vary is to change, or to be different.

[dict.2.vast]
Vast means very great in size.

[dict.2.vegetable]
A vegetable is a plant, or a part of a plant such as a root or a leaf, that people eat and that is not a fruit.

[dict.2.vegetation]
Vegetation is plants growing in a place.

[dict.2.veggie]
A veggie is a vegetable.

[dict.2.vehicle]
A vehicle is a thing that carries people from place to place.

[dict.2.velocity]
Velocity is speed in a direction.

[dict.2.verse]
A verse is a group of lines in a poem.

[dict.2.vertical]
Vertical means straight up and down.

[dict.2.vertically]
Vertically means straight up and down.

[dict.2.vessel]
A vessel is a ship, or a container for liquid.

[dict.2.via]
Via means by way of.

[dict.2.victory]
A victory is a win.

[dict.2.video]
A video is moving pictures, often with sound, that you can watch again and again.

[dict.2.vine]
A vine is a plant with a long, thin stem that grows along the ground or climbs up walls and trees.

[dict.2.violent]
Violent means using great force to hurt.

[dict.2.violet]
Violet is a color between blue and red, nearer to blue.

[dict.2.visibility]
Visibility is how far a person can see.

[dict.2.visible]
Visible means able to be seen.

[dict.2.visit]
To visit a person is to go see that person.

[dict.2.visual]
Visual means of the eyes.

[dict.2.vital]
Vital means necessary for life, or very important.

[dict.2.vocal]
Vocal means of the voice.

[dict.2.volcanic]
Volcanic means of a mountain that can throw out hot rock.

[dict.2.volcano]
A volcano is an opening in the ground, often on a mountain, where hot melted rock comes up from deep inside the earth.

[dict.2.volume]
Volume is how much space a thing fills.

[dict.2.volunteer]
A volunteer is a person who offers to do a thing without being made to, and to volunteer is to offer.

[dict.2.vorticity]
Vorticity is a measure of how much a flow is turning.

[dict.2.vote]
To vote is to show what you choose so that a group can decide.

[dict.2.wagon]
A wagon is a thing with wheels that is pulled and carries things.

[dict.2.wanderer]
A wanderer is a person who goes from place to place.

[dict.2.war]
A war is a time when countries or large groups of people use force to hurt each other and to win.

[dict.2.ward]
A ward is a person under the care of another.

[dict.2.warehouse]
A warehouse is a large building where things are kept.

[dict.2.warmth]
Warmth is the quality of being warm.

[dict.2.warn]
To warn a person is to tell the person of a danger.

[dict.2.waste]
Waste is a thing that people throw away.

[dict.2.wastewater]
Wastewater is dirty water after people use water.

[dict.2.watershed]
A watershed is land that sends water to one river.

[dict.2.wavelength]
A wavelength is the distance from one wave top to the next.

[dict.2.wealth]
Wealth is a large amount of what a person has.

[dict.2.web]
A web is what a small animal makes to catch food.

[dict.2.website]
A website is a set of pages on a computer that other people can read.

[dict.2.west]
West is the direction where the sun goes down in the evening.

[dict.2.western]
Western means of the west.

[dict.2.westward]
Westward means toward the west.

[dict.2.wetland]
A wetland is land that stays wet, like a marsh.

[dict.2.wetness]
Wetness is how wet a thing is.

[dict.2.whatever]
Whatever means any thing of the kind that is spoken of.

[dict.2.wherever]
Wherever means in any place.

[dict.2.whether]
Whether means if, when there are two things to choose from, such as yes or no.

[dict.2.whipcord]
Whipcord is thin hard rope.

[dict.2.whistle]
A whistle is a high sound made by blowing air through a small hole, or a small thing you blow to make that sound.

[dict.2.wicked]
Wicked means very bad on purpose.

[dict.2.widely]
Widely means over a wide area, or by many people.

[dict.2.widespread]
Widespread means found over a wide area.

[dict.2.widow]
A widow is a woman whose man has died.

[dict.2.width]
Width is how wide a thing is.

[dict.2.wife]
A wife is a woman a man has joined with.

[dict.2.wild]
Wild animals and plants live and grow on their own, not cared for by people.

[dict.2.willing]
Willing means ready to do a thing.

[dict.2.windborne]
Windborne means carried by the wind.

[dict.2.winter]
Winter is the cold part of the year.

[dict.2.wisdom]
Wisdom is knowing what is good to do.

[dict.2.wise]
Wise means knowing a lot and using it to choose well.

[dict.2.wish]
To wish is to want something very much, often something that may not happen.

[dict.2.within]
Within means inside something, or not past it.

[dict.2.witness]
A witness is a person who saw something, and to witness is to see it.

[dict.2.wolf]
A wolf is a wild animal that lives and catches food with others.

[dict.2.wonderful]
Wonderful means so good that it causes wonder.

[dict.2.wool]
Wool is the soft hair of a sheep, used to make cloth.

[dict.2.worldwide]
Worldwide means over the whole world.

[dict.2.worth]
If something is worth an amount, that is how much it is wanted or what it would cost.

[dict.2.wreck]
A wreck is what is left when a thing is broken.

[dict.2.yeast]
Yeast is a tiny living thing put into dough to make it rise, so that bread comes out soft when it is baked.

[dict.2.zonal]
Zonal means lying along an east and west line.

[dict.2.zone]
A zone is one part of a place and not the other parts.

[dict.2.zooplankton]
Zooplankton is the very small animals that float in the water.
